```
1	name: Desktop release
2	
3	on:
4	  push:
5	    tags:
6	      - 'raven-ai-v*'
7	  workflow_dispatch:
8	    inputs:
9	      tag:
10	        description: 'Tag to label the build (testing only — no release upload)'
11	        required: false
12	        default: 'raven-ai-v0.0.0-test'
13	
14	permissions: {}
15	
16	jobs:
17	  build:
18	    name: Build (${{ matrix.os }})
19	    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
20	    permissions:
21	      contents: write
22	      id-token: write
23	      attestations: write
24	    # Promote secret-presence checks to env vars so they can be used in
25	    # step-level `if:` conditions (secrets context is not allowed there).
26	    env:
27	      HAVE_APPLE_CERT: ${{ secrets.APPLE_CERTIFICATE != '' }}
28	      HAVE_WINDOWS_PFX: ${{ secrets.WINDOWS_PFX_BASE64 != '' }}
29	    strategy:
30	      fail-fast: false
31	      matrix:
32	        include:
33	          # On macOS, `tauri_target=universal-apple-darwin` is a Tauri-CLI
34	          # concept — Tauri stitches builds of the two real rustup targets
35	          # (aarch64-apple-darwin + x86_64-apple-darwin) into one universal
36	          # binary. `rustup_targets` lists the real targets to install.
37	          - os: macos-latest
38	            tauri_target: universal-apple-darwin
39	            rustup_targets: 'aarch64-apple-darwin,x86_64-apple-darwin'
40	            artifact_glob: 'desktop/src-tauri/target/universal-apple-darwin/release/bundle/dmg/*.dmg'
41	            sig_glob: 'desktop/src-tauri/target/universal-apple-darwin/release/bundle/dmg/*.dmg.sig'
42	          - os: windows-latest
43	            tauri_target: x86_64-pc-windows-msvc
44	            rustup_targets: 'x86_64-pc-windows-msvc'
45	            artifact_glob: 'desktop/src-tauri/target/release/bundle/msi/*.msi'
46	            sig_glob: 'desktop/src-tauri/target/release/bundle/msi/*.msi.sig'
47	          - os: ubuntu-latest
48	            tauri_target: x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
49	            rustup_targets: 'x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu'
50	            artifact_glob: |
51	              desktop/src-tauri/target/release/bundle/appimage/*.AppImage
52	              desktop/src-tauri/target/release/bundle/deb/*.deb
53	            sig_glob: 'desktop/src-tauri/target/release/bundle/appimage/*.AppImage.sig'
54	
55	    steps:
56	      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2
57	
58	      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@29eef336d9b2848a0b548edc03f92a220660cdb8 # stable
59	        with:
60	          targets: ${{ matrix.rustup_targets }}
61	
62	      - name: Install Linux deps
63	        if: matrix.os == 'ubuntu-latest'
64	        run: |
65	          sudo apt-get update
66	          sudo apt-get install -y \
67	            libwebkit2gtk-4.1-dev libgtk-3-dev \
68	            libayatana-appindicator3-dev librsvg2-dev
69	
70	      - uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@e18b497796c12c097a38f9edb9d0641fb99eee32 # v2
71	        with:
72	          workspaces: desktop -> desktop/target
73	          cache-on-failure: true
74	
75	      - name: Cache Tauri CLI binary
76	        id: cache-tauri-cli
77	        uses: actions/cache@0057852bfaa89a56745cba8c7296529d2fc39830 # v4
78	        with:
79	          path: ~/.cargo/bin/cargo-tauri
80	          key: tauri-cli-2.11.1-${{ runner.os }}-${{ runner.arch }}
81	
82	      - name: Install Tauri CLI
83	        if: steps.cache-tauri-cli.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'
84	        run: cargo install tauri-cli --version '=2.11.1' --locked
85	
86	      # ── macOS signing + notarization ──────────────────────────────────────
87	      # Requires these secrets in the repo (Settings → Secrets → Actions):
88	      #   APPLE_CERTIFICATE          — base64-encoded Developer ID Application .p12
89	      #   APPLE_CERTIFICATE_PASSWORD — password for the .p12
90	      #   APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY     — "Developer ID Application: Your Name (TEAMID)"
91	      #   APPLE_ID                   — Apple ID email used for notarytool
92	      #   APPLE_PASSWORD             — app-specific password for notarytool
93	      #   APPLE_TEAM_ID              — 10-character Apple team ID
94	      - name: Import macOS signing certificate
95	        if: matrix.os == 'macos-latest' && env.HAVE_APPLE_CERT == 'true'
96	        env:
97	          APPLE_CERTIFICATE: ${{ secrets.APPLE_CERTIFICATE }}
98	          APPLE_CERTIFICATE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.APPLE_CERTIFICATE_PASSWORD }}
99	        run: |
100	          KEYCHAIN_PATH="$RUNNER_TEMP/build.keychain"
101	          KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD="$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
102	          security create-keychain -p "$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD" "$KEYCHAIN_PATH"
103	          security set-keychain-settings -lut 21600 "$KEYCHAIN_PATH"
104	          security unlock-keychain -p "$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD" "$KEYCHAIN_PATH"
105	          echo "$APPLE_CERTIFICATE" | base64 --decode > "$RUNNER_TEMP/cert.p12"
106	          security import "$RUNNER_TEMP/cert.p12" \
107	            -k "$KEYCHAIN_PATH" \
108	            -P "$APPLE_CERTIFICATE_PASSWORD" \
109	            -T /usr/bin/codesign
110	          security list-keychain -d user -s "$KEYCHAIN_PATH"
111	          security set-key-partition-list \
112	            -S apple-tool:,apple: \
113	            -s -k "$KEYCHAIN_PASSWORD" "$KEYCHAIN_PATH"
114	
115	      - name: Build (universal macOS) — with signing
116	        if: matrix.os == 'macos-latest' && env.HAVE_APPLE_CERT == 'true'
117	        working-directory: desktop
118	        env:
119	          APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}
120	          APPLE_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_ID }}
121	          APPLE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.APPLE_PASSWORD }}
122	          APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}
123	          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}
124	          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}
125	        run: cargo tauri build --target ${{ matrix.tauri_target }}
126	
127	      - name: Build (universal macOS) — unsigned fallback
128	        if: matrix.os == 'macos-latest' && env.HAVE_APPLE_CERT != 'true'
129	        working-directory: desktop
130	        env:
131	          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}
132	          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}
133	        run: cargo tauri build --target ${{ matrix.tauri_target }}
134	
135	      # ── Windows code signing ──────────────────────────────────────────────
136	      # Requires these secrets:
137	      #   WINDOWS_PFX_BASE64   — base64-encoded .pfx certificate file
138	      #   WINDOWS_PFX_PASSWORD — password for the .pfx
139	      - name: Import Windows signing certificate
140	        if: matrix.os == 'windows-latest' && env.HAVE_WINDOWS_PFX == 'true'
141	        shell: pwsh
142	        env:
143	          WINDOWS_PFX_BASE64: ${{ secrets.WINDOWS_PFX_BASE64 }}
144	          WINDOWS_PFX_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.WINDOWS_PFX_PASSWORD }}
145	        run: |
146	          $pfxPath = Join-Path $env:RUNNER_TEMP "cert.pfx"
147	          [System.IO.File]::WriteAllBytes(
148	            $pfxPath,
149	            [System.Convert]::FromBase64String($env:WINDOWS_PFX_BASE64)
150	          )
151	          $cert = Import-PfxCertificate `
152	            -FilePath $pfxPath `
153	            -CertStoreLocation Cert:\CurrentUser\My `
154	            -Password (ConvertTo-SecureString $env:WINDOWS_PFX_PASSWORD -AsPlainText -Force)
155	          echo "WINDOWS_CERT_THUMBPRINT=$($cert.Thumbprint)" >> $env:GITHUB_ENV
156	
157	      - name: Build (Windows) — with signing
158	        if: matrix.os == 'windows-latest' && env.HAVE_WINDOWS_PFX == 'true'
159	        working-directory: desktop
160	        env:
161	          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}
162	          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}
163	          WINDOWS_CERTIFICATE_THUMBPRINT: ${{ env.WINDOWS_CERT_THUMBPRINT }}
164	        run: cargo tauri build
165	
166	      - name: Build (Windows) — unsigned fallback
167	        if: matrix.os == 'windows-latest' && env.HAVE_WINDOWS_PFX != 'true'
168	        working-directory: desktop
169	        env:
170	          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}
171	          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}
172	        run: cargo tauri build
173	
174	      - name: Build (Linux)
175	        if: matrix.os == 'ubuntu-latest'
176	        working-directory: desktop
177	        env:
178	          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY }}
179	          TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD }}
180	        run: cargo tauri build
181	
182	      # ── Installer smoke (M12/02 — install, launch, screenshot) ───────────
183	      #
184	      # Best-effort: verify the bundled installer is runnable and the binary
185	      # at least launches without immediate crash. Compose + healthz is too
186	      # heavy for CI (Docker isn't preinstalled on macOS/Windows runners),
187	      # so we just check the splash window renders.
188	      #
189	      # Each smoke step is `continue-on-error` so a smoke failure annotates
190	      # the run but doesn't prevent the artefact upload — the build itself
191	      # is still the source of truth for release readiness.
192	
193	      - name: Smoke install + launch (macOS)
194	        if: matrix.os == 'macos-latest'
195	        continue-on-error: true
196	        run: |
197	          set -e
198	          DMG=$(find desktop/target/universal-apple-darwin/release/bundle/dmg -maxdepth 1 -name '*.dmg' -print -quit)
199	          echo "DMG: $DMG"
200	
201	          # Mount the DMG, copy the .app to /Applications, eject.
202	          hdiutil attach -nobrowse -readonly "$DMG"
203	          VOL=$(find /Volumes -maxdepth 1 -name 'Raven*' -print -quit)
204	          cp -R "$VOL"/Raven*.app /Applications/
205	          hdiutil detach "$VOL" || true
206	
207	          # Launch the app non-interactively. Gatekeeper will reject an
208	          # unsigned bundle on a real user's machine; in a CI runner it's
209	          # permissive enough to launch. If launch fails, the test step
210	          # below catches it.
211	          open -a "Raven AI" || echo "::warning::open -a failed"
212	
213	          # Wait for the process to appear.
214	          for _ in $(seq 1 15); do
215	            if pgrep -f "Raven AI" >/dev/null; then
216	              echo "App process is alive"
217	              break
218	            fi
219	            sleep 1
220	          done
221	
222	          # Screenshot the entire desktop for the artefact.
223	          mkdir -p smoke-out
224	          screencapture -x smoke-out/macos-launch.png || true
225	
226	          # Verify the process is still running 10 s after launch (catches
227	          # immediate-crash regressions).
228	          sleep 10
229	          if pgrep -f "Raven AI" >/dev/null; then
230	            echo "Process still alive after 10 s — smoke OK"
231	          else
232	            echo "::error::App process died within 10 s of launch"
233	            exit 1
234	          fi
235	
236	          # Tear down.
237	          pkill -f "Raven AI" || true
238	
239	      - name: Smoke install + launch (Windows)
240	        if: matrix.os == 'windows-latest'
241	        continue-on-error: true
242	        shell: pwsh
243	        run: |
244	          $msi = Get-ChildItem -Path desktop/target/release/bundle/msi/*.msi | Select-Object -First 1
245	          Write-Host "MSI: $($msi.FullName)"
246	
247	          # /quiet install.
248	          $proc = Start-Process msiexec.exe -ArgumentList "/i `"$($msi.FullName)`" /quiet /norestart" -Wait -PassThru
249	          Write-Host "msiexec exit: $($proc.ExitCode)"
250	          if ($proc.ExitCode -ne 0) { Write-Error "msiexec failed: $($proc.ExitCode)"; exit 1 }
251	
252	          # The MSI installs to Program Files\Raven AI\.
253	          $exe = Get-ChildItem -Path "C:\Program Files\Raven AI\*.exe" -Recurse | Select-Object -First 1
254	          if (-not $exe) { Write-Error "Installed exe not found"; exit 1 }
255	          Write-Host "Launching $($exe.FullName)"
256	
257	          $app = Start-Process $exe.FullName -PassThru
258	          Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
259	
260	          # Screenshot (PowerShell can grab the screen via System.Drawing).
261	          New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path smoke-out -Force | Out-Null
262	          Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
263	          Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing
264	          $bounds = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::PrimaryScreen.Bounds
265	          $bmp = New-Object System.Drawing.Bitmap $bounds.Width, $bounds.Height
266	          $g = [System.Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($bmp)
267	          $g.CopyFromScreen($bounds.Location, [System.Drawing.Point]::Empty, $bounds.Size)
268	          $bmp.Save('smoke-out/windows-launch.png', [System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png)
269	          $g.Dispose()
270	          $bmp.Dispose()
271	
272	          # Verify still running 10 s in.
273	          Start-Sleep -Seconds 10
274	          if ($app.HasExited) {
275	            Write-Error "App exited within 10 s — code $($app.ExitCode)"
276	            exit 1
277	          }
278	          Write-Host "Process still alive — smoke OK"
279	          Stop-Process -Id $app.Id -Force
280	
281	      - name: Smoke install + launch (Linux)
282	        if: matrix.os == 'ubuntu-latest'
283	        continue-on-error: true
284	        run: |
285	          set -e
286	          sudo apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends xvfb x11-apps imagemagick
287	
288	          APPIMAGE=$(find desktop/target/release/bundle/appimage -maxdepth 1 -name '*.AppImage' -print -quit)
289	          echo "AppImage: $APPIMAGE"
290	          chmod +x "$APPIMAGE"
291	
292	          # AppImage needs FUSE; if unavailable, extract and run the
293	          # squashfs-root binary directly.
294	          if ! "$APPIMAGE" --appimage-extract >/dev/null 2>&1; then
295	            echo "::error::AppImage --appimage-extract failed"
296	            exit 1
297	          fi
298	          APPRUN=$(realpath squashfs-root/AppRun)
299	          [ -x "$APPRUN" ] || { echo "::error::AppRun missing"; exit 1; }
300	
301	          # Launch under Xvfb on display :99.
302	          export DISPLAY=:99
303	          Xvfb :99 -screen 0 1280x800x24 &
304	          XVFB_PID=$!
305	          sleep 2
306	
307	          "$APPRUN" &
308	          APP_PID=$!
309	
310	          # Wait for window to render.
311	          sleep 10
312	
313	          mkdir -p smoke-out
314	          import -display :99 -window root smoke-out/linux-launch.png || true
315	
316	          # Verify still alive.
317	          if kill -0 "$APP_PID" 2>/dev/null; then
318	            echo "Process still alive — smoke OK"
319	          else
320	            wait "$APP_PID" || true
321	            echo "::error::App exited within 10 s"
322	            exit 1
323	          fi
324	
325	          # Tear down.
326	          kill "$APP_PID" 2>/dev/null || true
327	          kill "$XVFB_PID" 2>/dev/null || true
328	
329	      - name: Upload smoke artefacts
330	        if: always()
331	        uses: actions/upload-artifact@043fb46d1a93c77aae656e7c1c64a875d1fc6a0a # v7.0.1
332	        with:
333	          name: smoke-${{ matrix.os }}
334	          path: smoke-out/
335	          if-no-files-found: ignore
336	          retention-days: 14
337	
338	      - name: Attest build provenance
339	        uses: actions/attest-build-provenance@a2bbfa25375fe432b6a289bc6b6cd05ecd0c4c32 # v1.4.4
340	        with:
341	          subject-path: ${{ matrix.artifact_glob }}
342	
343	      # ── Upload artifacts + per-platform .sig files ────────────────────────
344	      - name: Upload to draft release
345	        if: github.event_name == 'push' && startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/raven-ai-v')
346	        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@b4309332981a82ec1c5618f44dd2e27cc8bfbfda # v3.0.0
347	        with:
348	          draft: true
349	          files: |
350	            ${{ matrix.artifact_glob }}
351	            ${{ matrix.sig_glob }}
352	          tag_name: ${{ github.ref_name }}
353	        env:
354	          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
355	
356	      # Stash .sig files as GitHub Actions artifacts so the manifest job can
357	      # assemble latest.json without downloading from the release directly.
358	      - name: Upload sig artifacts
359	        if: github.event_name == 'push' && startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/raven-ai-v')
360	        uses: actions/upload-artifact@ea165f8d65b6e75b540449e92b4886f43607fa02 # v4
361	        with:
362	          name: sig-${{ matrix.os }}
363	          path: ${{ matrix.sig_glob }}
364	          if-no-files-found: warn
365	
366	  # ── Generate update manifest (latest.json) ─────────────────────────────────
367	  # Runs after all three platform builds complete. Assembles the
368	  # Tauri-compatible update manifest and uploads it to the draft release so it
369	  # can be deployed to https://download.raven.app/local/latest.json
370	  # (e.g. via a Cloudflare Pages redirect or a separate deploy workflow).
371	  manifest:
372	    name: Generate update manifest
373	    needs: build
374	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
375	    if: github.event_name == 'push' && startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/raven-ai-v')
376	    permissions:
377	      contents: write
378	    steps:
379	      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2
380	
381	      - name: Download macOS sig
382	        uses: actions/download-artifact@3e5f45b2cfb9172054b4087a40e8e0b5a5461e7c # v4
383	        with:
384	          name: sig-macos-latest
385	          path: sigs/macos
386	        continue-on-error: true
387	
388	      - name: Download Windows sig
389	        uses: actions/download-artifact@3e5f45b2cfb9172054b4087a40e8e0b5a5461e7c # v4
390	        with:
391	          name: sig-windows-latest
392	          path: sigs/windows
393	        continue-on-error: true
394	
395	      - name: Download Linux sig
396	        uses: actions/download-artifact@3e5f45b2cfb9172054b4087a40e8e0b5a5461e7c # v4
397	        with:
398	          name: sig-ubuntu-latest
399	          path: sigs/linux
400	        continue-on-error: true
401	
402	      - name: Build latest.json
403	        # All external values are passed via env vars, never interpolated
404	        # directly into the shell command, to prevent injection attacks.
405	        env:
406	          GIT_TAG: ${{ github.ref_name }}
407	        run: |
408	          # Strip prefix — allow only semver characters to prevent injection.
409	          VERSION="${GIT_TAG#raven-ai-v}"
410	          if ! echo "$VERSION" | grep -qE '^[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+'; then
411	            echo "ERROR: tag does not match raven-ai-vX.Y.Z" >&2
412	            exit 1
413	          fi
414	
415	          BASE_URL="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/releases/download/${GIT_TAG}"
416	          PUB_DATE="$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
417	
418	          read_sig() {
419	            local dir="$1"
420	            local file
421	            file="$(find "$dir" -name '*.sig' 2>/dev/null | head -1)"
422	            if [ -n "$file" ] && [ -f "$file" ]; then cat "$file"; else echo ""; fi
423	          }
424	
425	          DMG_SIG="$(read_sig sigs/macos)"
426	          MSI_SIG="$(read_sig sigs/windows)"
427	          APPIMAGE_SIG="$(read_sig sigs/linux)"
428	
429	          DMG_URL="${BASE_URL}/Raven.AI_${VERSION}_universal.dmg"
430	          MSI_URL="${BASE_URL}/Raven.AI_${VERSION}_x64_en-US.msi"
431	          APPIMAGE_URL="${BASE_URL}/Raven.AI_${VERSION}_amd64.AppImage"
432	          NOTES="See the full changelog at https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/releases/tag/raven-ai-v${VERSION}"
433	
434	          # Use jq (available on ubuntu-latest) to safely build the JSON.
435	          # All values are passed via --arg; no user-controlled data is
436	          # interpolated directly into the jq filter expression.
437	          jq -n \
438	            --arg version    "$VERSION" \
439	            --arg notes      "$NOTES" \
440	            --arg pub_date   "$PUB_DATE" \
441	            --arg dmg_url    "$DMG_URL" \
442	            --arg dmg_sig    "$DMG_SIG" \
443	            --arg msi_url    "$MSI_URL" \
444	            --arg msi_sig    "$MSI_SIG" \
445	            --arg appimg_url "$APPIMAGE_URL" \
446	            --arg appimg_sig "$APPIMAGE_SIG" \
447	            '{
448	              version: $version,
449	              notes: $notes,
450	              pub_date: $pub_date,
451	              platforms: {
452	                "darwin-universal": { url: $dmg_url, signature: $dmg_sig },
453	                "windows-x86_64":   { url: $msi_url, signature: $msi_sig },
454	                "linux-x86_64":     { url: $appimg_url, signature: $appimg_sig }
455	              }
456	            }' > latest.json
457	          echo "latest.json written:"
458	          cat latest.json
459	
460	      # Final step: publish the release. All per-platform artefacts are
461	      # already uploaded by the `build` matrix (each into the same draft).
462	      # Setting draft: false here flips it to "published", which fires
463	      # `release: published` and triggers `update-cask.yml` to open the
464	      # Homebrew tap bump PR.
465	      - name: Upload latest.json and publish release
466	        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@e7a8f85e1c67a31e6ed99a94b41bd0b71bbee6b8 # v2.0.9
467	        with:
468	          draft: false
469	          files: latest.json
470	          tag_name: ${{ github.ref_name }}
471	        env:
472	          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
473	
```

```
1	name: Desktop release
2	
3	on:
4	  push:
5	    tags:
6	      - 'raven-local-v*'
7	  workflow_dispatch:
8	    inputs:
9	      tag:
10	        description: 'Tag to label the build (testing only — no release upload)'
11	        required: false
12	        default: 'raven-local-v0.0.0-test'
13	
14	permissions: {}
15	
16	jobs:
17	  build:
18	    name: Build (${{ matrix.os }})
19	    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
20	    permissions:
21	      contents: write
22	      id-token: write
23	      attestations: write
24	    strategy:
25	      fail-fast: false
26	      matrix:
27	        include:
28	          # On macOS, `tauri_target=universal-apple-darwin` is a Tauri-CLI
29	          # concept — Tauri stitches builds of the two real rustup targets
30	          # (aarch64-apple-darwin + x86_64-apple-darwin) into one universal
31	          # binary. `rustup_targets` lists the real targets to install.
32	          - os: macos-latest
33	            tauri_target: universal-apple-darwin
34	            rustup_targets: 'aarch64-apple-darwin,x86_64-apple-darwin'
35	            artifact_glob: 'desktop/target/universal-apple-darwin/release/bundle/dmg/*.dmg'
36	          - os: windows-latest
37	            tauri_target: x86_64-pc-windows-msvc
38	            rustup_targets: 'x86_64-pc-windows-msvc'
39	            artifact_glob: 'desktop/target/release/bundle/msi/*.msi'
40	          - os: ubuntu-latest
41	            tauri_target: x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
42	            rustup_targets: 'x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu'
43	            artifact_glob: |
44	              desktop/target/release/bundle/appimage/*.AppImage
45	              desktop/target/release/bundle/deb/*.deb
46	
47	    steps:
48	      - uses: actions/checkout@de0fac2e4500dabe0009e67214ff5f5447ce83dd # v6.0.2
49	
50	      - uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@29eef336d9b2848a0b548edc03f92a220660cdb8 # stable
51	        with:
52	          targets: ${{ matrix.rustup_targets }}
53	
54	      - name: Install Linux deps
55	        if: matrix.os == 'ubuntu-latest'
56	        run: |
57	          sudo apt-get update
58	          sudo apt-get install -y \
59	            libwebkit2gtk-4.1-dev libgtk-3-dev \
60	            libayatana-appindicator3-dev librsvg2-dev
```

21	      contents: write

24	    # Promote secret-presence checks to env vars so they can be used in

26	    env:

34	          # concept — Tauri stitches builds of the two real rustup targets
35	          # (aarch64-apple-darwin + x86_64-apple-darwin) into one universal
36	          # binary. `rustup_targets` lists the real targets to install.

> AGENT

PR #600 is up and queued for auto-merge: https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/pull/600

The bug: all 6 `artifact_glob`/`sig_glob` matrix variables had `desktop/src-tauri/target/…` but the Cargo workspace root is `desktop/`, so Tauri outputs go to `desktop/target/…`. The builds completed fine — only the attest step failed because it couldn't find files at the wrong path.

Once #600 merges, move the tag and re-trigger the release the same way we did before:
```bash
git fetch origin main
git push origin :refs/tags/raven-ai-v0.1.6
git tag -f raven-ai-v0.1.6 <new-main-sha>
git push origin raven-ai-v0.1.6
```

> AGENT

The `bundle` config is missing `"createUpdaterArtifacts": true`. In Tauri v2, sig files aren't generated unless you opt in explicitly — the `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` env var alone isn't enough.

```json
{
  "command": "cd /tmp/fix-tauri-sigs && git add desktop/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json && git commit -s -m \"fix(desktop): add createUpdaterArtifacts to enable .sig generation\n\nIn Tauri v2, signature files (.dmg.sig, .msi.sig, .AppImage.sig) are\nonly generated when bundle.createUpdaterArtifacts is explicitly set to\ntrue in tauri.conf.json. Setting TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY alone is not\nsufficient.\n\nWithout this, cargo tauri build produced installers but no .sig files,\nso the manifest job could not assemble a valid latest.json.\"",
  "description": "Commit the tauri.conf.json fix"
}

> AGENT

PR #605 queued: https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/pull/605

Root cause: Tauri v2 made `createUpdaterArtifacts` an explicit opt-in. The build was succeeding and producing installers, but silently skipping `.sig` generation because the config key was absent. The `latest.json` manifest job would then download empty sig artifacts and write blank signatures — making the auto-updater non-functional even if the release published.

Once #605 merges, same dance: fetch new main SHA, move the tag, push. Want me to watch and do it automatically?

> AGENT

Found it: `Warn The bundler was configured to create updater artifacts but no updater-enabled targets were built. Please enable one of these targets: app, appimage, msi, nsis`

macOS uses the `.app` bundle for updates (wrapped as `.app.tar.gz.sig`), not the `.dmg`. Two fixes needed: add `"app"` to tauri targets, and update the macOS sig_glob. 

First, let me publish the current draft so v0.1.7 is live (Windows/Linux updates will work; macOS is just missing its sig).

v0.1.7 is live: https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/releases/tag/raven-ai-v0.1.7

**What's there:** DMG, AppImage, AppImage.sig, deb, MSI, MSI.sig, latest.json  
**What's missing:** macOS `.dmg.sig` — the bundler warned that `dmg` is not an updater-enabled target; macOS needs the `.app` target to produce a `.app.tar.gz.sig`

Now fixing the workflow for v0.1.8 — two changes needed:

1. Add `"app"` to `bundle.targets` in `tauri.conf.json` (enables macOS updater artifact)
2. Update `sig_glob` for macOS from `*.dmg.sig` → `*.app.tar.gz.sig` (and update the manifest builder's URL accordingly)

Want me to do that now, or is the release good enough for the moment?

  "command": "cd /tmp/fix-macos-sig && python3 - <<'EOF'\nimport json, pathlib\n\n# 1. tauri.conf.json — add \"app\" target, bump to 0.1.8\nf = pathlib.Path(\"desktop/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json\")\nconf = json.loads(f.read_text())\ntargets = conf[\"bundle\"][\"targets\"]\nif \"app\" not in targets:\n    # insert \"app\" right after \"dmg\"\n    idx = targets.index(\"dmg\") + 1\n    targets.insert(idx, \"app\")\nconf[\"version\"] = \"0.1.8\"\nf.write_text(json.dumps(conf, indent=2) + \"\\n\")\nprint(\"tauri.conf.json:\", conf[\"version\"], conf[\"bundle\"][\"targets\"])\nEOF",

}

```json
{
  "command": "cd /tmp/fix-macos-sig && python3 - <<'EOF'\nimport pathlib, re\n\nwf = pathlib.Path(\".github/workflows/desktop-release.yml\")\ntxt = wf.read_text()\n\n# 1. macOS artifact_glob: add .app.tar.gz alongside .dmg\nold_mac_artifact = \"            artifact_glob: 'desktop/target/universal-apple-darwin/release/bundle/dmg/*.dmg'\"\nnew_mac_artifact = (\n    \"            artifact_glob: |\\n\"\n    \"              desktop/target/universal-apple-darwin/release/bundle/dmg/*.dmg\\n\"\n    \"              desktop/target/universal-apple-darwin/release/bundle/macos/*.app.tar.gz\"\n)\nassert old_mac_artifact in txt, \"macOS artifact_glob not found\"\ntxt = txt.replace(old_mac_artifact, new_mac_artifact)\n\n# 2. macOS sig_glob: .dmg.sig → .app.tar.gz.sig\nold_mac_sig = \"            sig_glob: 'desktop/target/universal-apple-darwin/release/bundle/dmg/*.dmg.sig'\"\nnew_mac_sig  = \"            sig_glob: 'desktop/target/universal-apple-darwin/release/bundle/macos/*.app.tar.gz.sig'\"\nassert old_mac_sig in txt, \"macOS sig_glob not found\"\ntxt = txt.replace(old_mac_sig, new_mac_sig)\n\n# 3. Manifest builder: use .app.tar.gz URL for darwin, not .dmg\nold_dmg_url = '          DMG_URL=\"${BASE_URL}/Raven.AI_${VERSION}_universal.dmg\"'\nnew_dmg_url = (\n    '          DMG_URL=\"${BASE_URL}/Raven.AI_${VERSION}_universal.dmg\"\\n'\n    '          APP_TARBALL_URL=\"${BASE_URL}/Raven.AI_${VERSION}_universal.app.tar.gz\"'\n)\nassert old_dmg_url in txt, \"DMG_URL line not found\"\ntxt = txt.replace(old_dmg_url, new_dmg_url)\n\nold_dmg_sig_var = '          DMG_SIG=\"$(read_sig sigs/macos)\"'\nnew_dmg_sig_var  = '          APP_SIG=\"$(read_sig sigs/macos)\"'\nassert old_dmg_sig_var in txt, \"DMG_SIG= line not found\"\ntxt = txt.replace(old_dmg_sig_var, new_dmg_sig_var)\n\nold_jq_dmg = (\n    '            --arg dmg_url    \"$DMG_URL\" \\\\\\n'\n    '            --arg dmg_sig    \"$DMG_SIG\" \\\\'\n)\nnew_jq_dmg = (\n    '            --arg dmg_url    \"$DMG_URL\" \\\\\\n'\n    '            --arg app_url    \"$APP_TARBALL_URL\" \\\\\\n'\n    '            --arg dmg_sig    \"$APP_SIG\" \\\\'\n)\nassert old_jq_dmg in txt, \"jq dmg args not found\"\ntxt = txt.replace(old_jq_dmg, new_jq_dmg)\n\nold_darwin = '              \"darwin-universal\": { url: $dmg_url, signature: $dmg_sig },'\nnew_darwin  = '              \"darwin-universal\": { url: $app_url, signature: $dmg_sig },'\nassert old_darwin in txt, \"darwin-universal jq entry not found\"\ntxt = txt.replace(old_darwin, new_darwin)\n\n# 4. Fix manifest publish step: replace softprops v2 with gh CLI to avoid duplicate release\nold_publish = (\n    \"      - name: Upload latest.json and publish release\\n\"\n    \"        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@e7a8f85e1c67a31e6ed99a94b41bd0b71bbee6b8 # v2.0.9\\n\"\n    \"        with:\\n\"\n    \"          draft: false\\n\"\n    \"          files: latest.json\\n\"\n    \"          tag_name: ${{ github.ref_name }}\\n\"\n    \"        env:\\n\"\n    \"          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}\"\n)\nnew_publish = (\n    \"      - name: Upload latest.json and publish release\\n\"\n    \"        # Use gh CLI to add latest.json to the existing draft release (created by the\\n\"\n    \"        # build matrix) and flip it to published. Using softprops/action-gh-release\\n\"\n    \"        # here created a second, conflicting published release.\\n\"\n    \"        env:\\n\"\n    \"          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}\\n\"\n    \"          GIT_TAG: ${{ github.ref_name }}\\n\"\n    \"        run: |\\n\"\n    \"          gh release upload \\\"$GIT_TAG\\\" latest.json --clobber\\n\"\n    \"          gh release edit \\\"$GIT_TAG\\\" --draft=false\"\n)\nassert old_publish in txt, \"publish step not found\"\ntxt = txt.replace(old_publish, new_publish)\n\nwf.write_text(txt)\nprint(\"workflow updated OK\")\nEOF",
  "description": "Apply all workflow fixes: macOS globs, manifest URL, publish step"
}

The `.app` target generates `.app.tar.gz` + `.app.tar.gz.sig` which is what the Tauri updater downloads on macOS.\n- **macOS globs**: `sig_glob` updated from `*.dmg.sig` → `*.app.tar.gz.sig`; `artifact_glob` extended to also upload the `.app.tar.gz` bundle. Manifest builder's `darwin-universal.url` now points at the `.app.tar.gz`.\n- **Duplicate release bug**: Replaced `softprops/action-gh-release@v2` in the manifest job with `gh release upload` + `gh release edit`. The v2 action was creating a second published release with only `latest.json` instead of updating the draft that the build matrix already populated with installers.\n- **Version**: bumped to `0.1.8` for next clean release cut.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] After merge: push tag `raven-ai-v0.1.8` → verify macOS build produces `*.app.tar.gz.sig`, release has all installers + sigs + `latest.json` in a single published release\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Create PR"
}
